            }
            
            const modal = document.getElementById('chat-options-modal');
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            setTimeout(() => modal.classList.add('modal-open'), 10);
        }

        function closeChatOptionsModal() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('chat-options-modal');
            modal.classList.remove('modal-open');
            setTimeout(() => { modal.classList.add('hidden'); }, 300);
        }

        function actionRename() {
            if (!activeOptionsSession) return;
            const { charName } = activeOptionsSession;
            closeChatOptionsModal();
            
            // Заполняем инпут старым именем и показываем модалку
            const input = document.getElementById('rename-input');
            input.value = charName;
            
            const modal = document.getElementById('rename-modal');
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            setTimeout(() => { 
                modal.classList.add('modal-open');
                input.focus(); // Пытаемся сразу открыть клавиатуру
            }, 10);
        }

        function closeRenameModal() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('rename-modal');
            modal.classList.remove('modal-open');
            setTimeout(() => modal.classList.add('hidden'), 300);
        }

        function submitRename() {
            if (!activeOptionsSession) return;
            const { sessionId } = activeOptionsSession;
            const newName = document.getElementById('rename-input').value.trim();

            if (!newName) {
                tg.HapticFeedback.notificationOccurred('error');
                return showToast(translations[currentLang].toast_name_empty, "error");
            }

            if (newName.length > 50) {
                tg.HapticFeedback.notificationOccurred('error');
                return showToast(translations[currentLang].rename_limit, "error");
            }

            closeRenameModal();
            tg.MainButton.showProgress();

            fetch(WEBHOOK_CHAT_ACTION, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ 
                    action: 'rename', 
                    session_id: sessionId, 
                    new_name: newName, 
                    chat_id: tg.initDataUnsafe?.user?.id, 
                    initData: tg.initData 
                })
            })
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                tg.MainButton.hideProgress();
                if (data.success) { 
                    showToast(translations[currentLang].toast_chat_renamed, "success"); 
                    fetchChatsList(); 
                } else {
                    showToast(translations[currentLang].toast_db_error, "error");
                }
            })
            .catch(() => { 
                tg.MainButton.hideProgress(); 
                showToast(translations[currentLang].toast_conn_error, "error");
            });
        }

        function actionPin() {
            if (!activeOptionsSession) return;
            const { sessionId, isPinned } = activeOptionsSession;
            closeChatOptionsModal();
            tg.MainButton.showProgress();

            fetch(WEBHOOK_CHAT_ACTION, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'pin', session_id: sessionId, is_pinned: !isPinned, chat_id: tg.initDataUnsafe?.user?.id, initData: tg.initData })
            }).then(res => res.json()).then(data => {
                tg.MainButton.hideProgress();
                if (data.success) { 
                    showToast(!isPinned ? translations[currentLang].toast_chat_pinned : translations[currentLang].toast_chat_unpinned, "success"); 
                    fetchChatsList(); 
                }
            }).catch(() => { tg.MainButton.hideProgress(); });
        }

        // --- ЛОГИКА УДАЛЕНИЯ ЧАТА (ЧЕРЕЗ КРАСИВУЮ МОДАЛКУ) ---
        function actionDelete() {
            if (!activeOptionsSession) return;
            
            // Закрываем меню чата (где три точки)
            closeChatOptionsModal();
            
            // Восстанавливаем кнопку удаления (вдруг она зависла на "Удаление...")
            const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-delete-btn');
            const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('cancel-delete-btn');
            const btnSpan = confirmBtn.querySelector('span');
            const btnIcon = confirmBtn.querySelector('i');

            confirmBtn.disabled = false;
            cancelBtn.classList.remove('opacity-50', 'pointer-events-none');
            btnIcon.className = "fa-solid fa-trash-can";
            btnSpan.innerText = translations[currentLang]?.delete_confirm || 'Yes, delete';
            confirmBtn.classList.remove('bg-gray-600', 'cursor-not-allowed');
            confirmBtn.classList.add('bg-red-600');

            // Открываем наше красивое окно
            const modal = document.getElementById('delete-modal');
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            setTimeout(() => modal.classList.add('modal-open'), 10);
        }

        function closeDeleteModal() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('delete-modal');
            modal.classList.remove('modal-open');
            setTimeout(() => modal.classList.add('hidden'), 300);
        }

        function confirmDeleteChat() {
            if (!activeOptionsSession) return;
            const { sessionId } = activeOptionsSession;

            // Включаем спиннер загрузки на кнопке
            const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-delete-btn');
            const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('cancel-delete-btn');
            const btnSpan = confirmBtn.querySelector('span');
            const btnIcon = confirmBtn.querySelector('i');

            confirmBtn.disabled = true;
            cancelBtn.classList.add('opacity-50', 'pointer-events-none');
            btnIcon.className = "fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin";
            btnSpan.innerText = translations[currentLang]?.deleting_process || 'Deleting...';
            confirmBtn.classList.remove('bg-red-600');
            confirmBtn.classList.add('bg-gray-600', 'cursor-not-allowed');

            // Запрос на бэкенд
            fetch(WEBHOOK_CHAT_ACTION, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'delete', session_id: sessionId, chat_id: tg.initDataUnsafe?.user?.id, initData: tg.initData })
            }).then(res => res.json()).then(data => {
                if (data.success) {
                    closeDeleteModal();
                    showToast(translations[currentLang].toast_chat_deleted, "success");
                    fetchChatsList();
                } else {
                    tg.HapticFeedback.notificationOccurred('error');
                    closeDeleteModal();
                    showToast(translations[currentLang].toast_delete_error, "error");
                }
            }).catch(() => {
                tg.HapticFeedback.notificationOccurred('error');
                closeDeleteModal();
                showToast(translations[currentLang].toast_conn_error, "error");
            });
        }
        
        function renderReferrals(referrals) {
            const listContainer = document.getElementById('referrals-list');
            document.getElementById('ref-count').innerText = referrals.length;

            // Если список пустой
            if (referrals.length === 0) {
                listContainer.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full opacity-40 pt-10">
                        <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-white/5 flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-user-xmark text-xl"></i>
                        </div>
                        <p class="text-xs text-gray-300 font-medium" data-i18n="no_friends_title">${translations[currentLang].no_friends_title}</p>
                        <p class="text-[10px] text-gray-500 text-center mt-1" data-i18n="no_friends_desc">${translations[currentLang].no_friends_desc}</p>
                    </div>
                `;
                return;
            }

            // Если друзья есть - рисуем карточки со Звездами и Гемами
            listContainer.innerHTML = referrals.map(ref => `
                <div class="bg-[#1a1a1a] rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center justify-between border border-white/5">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 overflow-hidden">
                        <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-tr from-blue-500 to-purple-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shrink-0">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-user"></i>
                        </div>
                        <div class="overflow-hidden">
                            <div class="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate">${(ref.name || 'Игрок ' + ref.id).replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')}</div>
                            <div class="text-[9px] text-gray-500 font-mono truncate">ID: ${ref.id}</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 pl-2">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-1 bg-yellow-500/10 px-1.5 py-1 rounded-md border border-yellow-500/20">
                            <span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-yellow-400">+20</span>
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-star text-[8px] text-yellow-500"></i>
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-1 bg-cyan-500/10 px-1.5 py-1 rounded-md border border-cyan-500/20">
                            <span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-cyan-400">+3</span>
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-gem text-[8px] text-cyan-500"></i>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `).join('');
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

        let currentLang = 'ru';

        // Автоопределение языка браузера при загрузке
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            const browserLang = navigator.language || navigator.userLanguage;
            if (browserLang && !browserLang.toLowerCase().startsWith('ru')) {
                currentLang = 'en';
            }
            updateLanguageUI();
        });

        function toggleLanguage() {
            currentLang = currentLang === 'ru' ? 'en' : 'ru';
            updateLanguageUI();
        }

        function updateLanguageUI() {
            document.getElementById('lang-text').innerText = currentLang === 'ru' ? 'RU' : 'EN';
            
            // Words for the typing effect
            const wordsRu = ["девушка", "госпожа", "подруга", "напарница"];
            const wordsEn = ["girl", "mistress", "friend", "partner"];
            words = currentLang === 'ru' ? wordsRu : wordsEn;
            
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el => {
                const key = el.getAttribute('data-i18n');
                if (translations[currentLang][key]) {
                    el.innerHTML = translations[currentLang][key];
                }
            });
        }

        // ==========================================
        // UI & ANIMATIONS
        // ==========================================
        let words = ["девушка", "госпожа", "подруга", "напарница"];
        let i = 0;
        let timer;
        const target = document.getElementById('typing-text');

        function typingEffect() {
            let word = words[i].split('');
            var loopTyping = function() {
                if (word.length > 0) {
                    target.innerHTML += word.shift();
                } else {
                    setTimeout(deletingEffect, 2000);
                    return false;
                }
                timer = setTimeout(loopTyping, 100);
            };
            loopTyping();
        }

        function deletingEffect() {
            let word = words[i].split('');
            var loopDeleting = function() {
                if (word.length > 0) {
                    word.pop();
                    target.innerHTML = word.join('');
                } else {
                    if (words.length > (i + 1)) {
                        i++;
                    } else {
                        i = 0;
                    }
                    typingEffect();
                    return false;
                }
                timer = setTimeout(loopDeleting, 50);
            };
            loopDeleting();
        }
        setTimeout(typingEffect, 1000);

        document.querySelectorAll('.faq-btn').forEach(button => {
            button.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const content = button.nextElementSibling;
                const icon = button.querySelector('i');
                document.querySelectorAll('.faq-content').forEach(c => {
                    if (c !== content) {
                        c.classList.add('hidden');
                        c.previousElementSibling.querySelector('i').style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
                    }
                });
                if (content.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                    content.classList.remove('hidden');
                    icon.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)';
                } else {
                    content.classList.add('hidden');
                    icon.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
                }
            });
        });

        window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
            const nav = document.getElementById('navbar');
            if (window.scrollY > 50) {
                nav.classList.add('bg-black/80', 'backdrop-blur-md', 'border-b', 'border-white/10');
            } else {
                nav.classList.remove('bg-black/80', 'backdrop-blur-md', 'border-b', 'border-white/10');
            }
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
